# Beating Heart
Aliases: Bloody
Categories: Rare items, Power stones, Mob Drops, Power Stones, Dungeon Items
[Beating Heart]
Type: Accessory Power Stone
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Power: Bloody
Req. Skill Level: Combat XX (20) ([[Combat]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BEATING_HEART
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Bloody power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +348.97&#xE00D; Strength +348.97&#xE007; Crit Damage +116.32&#xE003; Intelligence; Unique Power Bonus:; +10&#xE001; Attack Speed; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 20.; RARE POWER STONE
The Beating Heart is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Bloody]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Strength]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Crit Damage]]. It also provides +10 [[Attack Speed]] as its unique power bonus.

## Requirements
The Beating Heart requires [[Combat|Combat XX (20)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Beating Hearts at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Bloody [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

# Beetle Shard
Aliases: Crop Bug, Crop bug, Cropbeetle, Cropeetle, Cropeetle Shard
Categories: Rare items, Shards
[Beetle Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: R16
Families: Bug ([[Shard Families]]), Trash Lover ([[Shard Families]])
Skill: Farming
Category: Forest
[Attribute]
Name: Crop Bug ([[Attributes]])
Effect: Grants +1–10 [[Overbloom]] which increases your chances of dropping [[Rare Crops]].
Requirement: Hunting X (10) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: CROPEETLE
Tooltip: Crop Bug I Grants +1  Overbloom which increases your chances of dropping RARE CROPS.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE FOREST SHARD (ID R16)
The Beetle Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Crop Bug]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Beetle Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Beetle]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Beetle Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Beetle Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Crop Bug]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Crop Bug ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Overbloom]] which increases your chances of dropping [[Rare Crops]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Beetle Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Beetle Shard has the following description:
- These insects are known for their strong mandibles, which they use to cut through plant stems and leaves.

# Bejeweled Collar
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pet items, Dwarven Mines
[Bejeweled Collar]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 3x [[Glacite Jewel]] 102,400x (1,600 stacks (102,400 items total)) [[Mithril]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BEJEWELED_COLLAR
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +25 Mining Speed and +10 Mining Fortune.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; RARE PET ITEM
A Bejeweled Collar is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Bejeweled Collar will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +25 [[Mining Speed]] and +10 [[Mining Fortune]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Bejeweled Collar for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Obtaining
A Bejeweled Collar can be [[The Forge|forged]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It takes 2 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Bejeweled Collar]] 1 Bejeweled Collar
  - 4 [[Refined Mithril]] 640 (10 stacks) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 102,400 (1,600 stacks) [[Mithril]]
  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Added Bejeweled Collar.|

# Bejeweled Handle
Categories: Rare items, Items, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
[Bejeweled Handle]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Heart of the Mountain II (2) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 3x [[Glacite Jewel]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BEJEWELED_HANDLE
Tooltip: RARE
Bejeweled Handle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Bejeweled Handle can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 3 [[Glacite Jewel|Glacite Jewels]] and 30 seconds to forge.
It can also be obtained from looting [[Frozen Corpses]].
### Forging Tree
- [[Bejeweled Handle]] 1 Bejeweled Handle
  - 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]

## Trivia
- Bejeweled Handles cannot be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]. If one is already recombobulated, it will be forcibly reverted to its base rarity.
  - This item was often recombobulated and put on the [[Auction House]] to appear like a [[Necron's Handle]] before it became a [[Bazaar]] item. See Tutorial:Avoiding scams for more information.

# Berberis Fuel Injector
Categories: Rare items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Wilted Berberis Collection, Minion Modifiers
[Berberis Fuel Injector]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Wilted Berberis IV (4) ([[Wilted Berberis]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 336x (5 stacks plus 16 (336 in total)) [[Wilted Berberis]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BERBERIS_FUEL_INJECTOR
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade, only for farming minions.; Increases the speed of your minion by 15%.; Your minion will occasionally generate Lush Berberis items.; X Rift-Transferable X RARE
The Berberis Fuel Injector is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wilted Berberis IV (4) ([[Wilted Berberis]])|336x [[Wilted Berberis]]|Recipe: [[Wilted Berberis]] x48 ×7 → Berberis Fuel Injector|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Berberis Fuel Injector can be placed in farming [[Minions]], see below. It increases minion speed by +15%, making it second best minion upgrade for minion speed. It also makes the minion produce 1 [[Lush Berberis]] every 5 minutes in addition to the items the minion normally produces.
- Farming Minions
- [[Wheat Minion]]
- [[Carrot Minion]]
- [[Cocoa Beans Minion]]
- [[Mushroom Minion]]
- [[Sheep Minion]]
- [[Cactus Minion]]
- [[Cow Minion]]
- [[Pig Minion]]
- [[Potato Minion]]
- [[Pumpkin Minion]]
- [[Melon Minion]]
- [[Rabbit Minion]]
- [[Chicken Minion]]
- [[Nether Wart Minion]]
- [[Sugar Cane Minion]]
- [[Sunflower Minion]]
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Berberis Fuel Injector can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. This must be done so that the Berberis Fuel Injector can actually be used.
## Trivia
- Minions will wait until the next production cycle to generate [[Lush Berberis]] if the player is on the island.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Berberis Fuel Injector.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Berberis Fuel Injector.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Berberis Fuel Injector not working in Chicken Minions ([[Chicken Minion]]).|

The Billygoat Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Frozen Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Billygoat Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Billygoat]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Billygoat Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Billygoat Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Frozen Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Frozen Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] on Frozen ([[Frozen (Mob Type)]]) mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Billygoat Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Billygoat Shard has the following description:
- The Billygoat stands alone atop the summit of the tallest mountain. Nothing else comes close and, should it try to, it'll be sent back to earth courtesy of a particularly harsh headbutt.

Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

Buy: 150 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]); 1 [[Bingo Ring]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BINGO_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; B.I.N.G.O.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
Bingo Artifact is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]], purchasable from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]].
## Obtaining
Bingo Artifact can be purchased from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 1 [[Bingo Ring]] and 150 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3).
## Usage
[[Bingo Relic]] can be bought from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] with 1x [[Bingo Artifact]] Bingo Artifact and 200 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4).
## Trivia
- Alongside [[Hegemony Artifact]] and [[Hocus-Pocus Cipher]] (outside of the rift) it does not grant any effects, which means it can only grant boosts through [[Powers|Accessory Powers]].
- The lore of the item is a reference to the children's song BINGO, where the letters of the word are sung to spell out the name of a farmer's dog.
  - Furthermore, when playing the game Bingo, columns are often distinguished by those letters as opposed to A, B, C...
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 29, 2021|Added Bingo Artifact.|
|May 29, 2022|Bingo Artifact becomes obtainable.|
|Jan 28, 2022|Bingo Artifact is no longer Co-op Soulbound.|

[Biofuel]
Type: [[Machine Fuel]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])

Tooltip: Machine Fuel; Adds +3,000 Fuel to a refuelable machine.; 100% renewable energy!; RARE
Biofuel is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Machine Fuel]].
## Obtaining
Biofuel is sold by [[Bubu]] and [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 5,000 [[Coins]].
It can also be obtained as a bonus reward from the [[Geonathan Greatforge]] and [[Jotraeline Greatforge]] [[Garden Visitors]].
## Usage
Biofuel adds 3,000 Fuel to [[Drills]] when combined in a [[Drill Anvil]], or to the [[Composter]] when inserted.
## Tips
- It is generally much cheaper and more efficient to use [[Oil Barrel]] or [[Volta]], or [[Sunflower Oil]] instead. However, Biofuel is a convenient option for early game [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] players that can be bought for [[Coins]] rather than having to grind for it.

The Bitbug Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cookie Eater]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Bitbug Shard can be purchased from [[Elizabeth]]'s [[Bits Shop]] for 5,000 [[Bits]].
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bitbug Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cookie Eater]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cookie Eater ([[Attributes]]); 5%–50% of your [[Booster Cookie]] [[Bits]] are gained up front.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Bits Talisman
Categories: Rare items, Accessories, Bits Shop, Talisman
[Bits Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 15,000 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BITS_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Whenever you gain bits, grants a 10%; chance to gain double!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Bits Talisman is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants a 10% chance to earn double the amount of [[Bits]] a player would have normally received.
## Obtaining
|Merchant|Price x1|
|-|-|
|[[Elizabeth]]|15,000 [[Bits]]|
## Usage
While in the player's inventory, or inside of the [[Accessory Bag]], the Bits Talisman will grant a 10% chance to earn double the amount of [[Bits]] the player would have earned. This includes through [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]], Slaying [[Bosses]], and completing [[Dungeons]]. See [[Bits]] for more information.
The [[Bits]] earned from this talisman will still count toward the total [[Bits]] to be earned from a [[Booster Cookie]]. The player will just get [[Bits]] faster per cookie (about 9.09% faster), not more per [[Booster Cookie]].
## Trivia
- For a very short period of time after it was added, this talisman was placeable on a [[Private Island]].
- This is the tenth-most expensive item to buy from [[Elizabeth]] using [[Bits]]; the most expensive 2 being [[Pure Black Dye]] or [[Pure White Dye]] at 250,000 [[Bits]] each.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added Bits Talisman.|
|Sep 8, 2020|The Bits Talisman can no longer be placed on Private Islands ([[Private Island]]).|

# Blastberry
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Blastberry]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLASTBERRY
Tooltip: Only harvest at your own risk.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Blastberry is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Blastberry can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 5 [[Chocoberry]] and 3 [[Ashwreath]] within 1 block of [[Sand]].
While growing, a Blastberry needs water and has 6 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it grants immunity to negative effects, improves yield by 30%, and reduces [[Farming]] experience by -20%.
When a Blastberry is harvested or decays, it explodes, destroying adjacent crops 1 block away. If there is another Blastberry in range, it is ignored and not destroyed.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Blastberries also drop 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Cocoa Beans]], 1,200 (18 stacks plus 48) [[Nether Wart]], and +3,200 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Blastberry can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 240,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 120 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Blastberry grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Blastberries are used to create a [[Shellfruit]] and [[Startlevine]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone V (5) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) 8x [[Cheesebite]] [[Blastberry]] 8x Blastberry 16x [[Fleshtrap]]|Recipe: [[Fleshtrap]] x4 ×4 + Blastberry x4 ×2 + [[Cheesebite]] x4 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
## Trivia
- Despite reducing [[Farming]] experience by -20%, the effect is nullified due to granting immunity to negative effects.
## Gallery
- Blastberry in the Greenhouse Blastberry in the [[Greenhouse]]

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Blastberry.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Blastberry.|

Blaze Wax is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Waxed]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rod VI (6)]]. It grants [[Health]] and [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Rod VI (6) ([[Blaze Rod]])|10x [[Bezos]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] x10 ×8 + [[Bezos]] x10 → Blaze Wax|
## Usage
### Reforging
Blaze Wax can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Waxed]] reforge, to grant [[Health]] and [[Crit Chance]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Waxed]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

A Blazen Sphere is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Blazing]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. It grants [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Heat Resistance]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv123]|[[Lava Blaze]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Blazen Sphere can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Blazing]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Fortune]] and [[Heat Resistance]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXVII (27)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Blazing]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Blazing Bonus, which grants 0.05% extra chance to find [[Stoneworm|Worms]] for every [[Heat]] the player has.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLESSED_FRUIT
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Blessed reforge when combined with a Farming Tool.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Farming Fortune  Speed ☯ Farming Wisdom; Blessed Bonus You have a 0.25% chance to find enchanted forms of items when harvesting Crops.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; RARE REFORGE STONE
A Blessed Fruit is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Blessed]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]], [[Speed]] and [[Farming Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
A Blessed Fruit can purchased from the [[Smithmonger]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Blessed Fruit can be combined with a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Blessed]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]], [[Speed]] and [[Farming Wisdom]], which scale with the Farming Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]]. A Farming Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Blessed]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Blessed Bonus, which grants 0.25% (1 in 400) chance to find enchanted forms of items when harvesting crops.

## Trivia
- The [[Speed]] bonus used to be applied for each unique hoe in the inventory.
- Before the addition of [[Farming Wisdom]], the farming exp bonus from the blessed reforge was additive with the [[Rabbit Pet]]'s Farming Exp Boost perk and [[Derpy]]'s MOAR SKILLZ!!! perk unlike other skill bonuses.
- When the Blessed bonus activates, the following message is sent in chat:
BLESSED! You found an <enchanted item>!

# Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin
Aliases: Blizzard in a Bottle
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items
[Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✕ No

Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 48x [[Hunk of Ice]] 4x [[Walnut]] 1x [[True Ice]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 True Ice)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLIZZARD_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Increases the effects of Weather by 10%.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
A Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[True Ice]] 4x [[Walnut]] 48x [[Hunk of Ice]]|Recipe: [[Walnut]] x4 + [[Hunk of Ice]] x16 ×3 + [[True Ice]] → Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin|
## Usage
### Brewing
A Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to increase all effects of [[Weather]] by 10%. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Blizzard in a Bottle Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.

The Blood Donor Artifact is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Hemovibe IX (9) ([[Hemovibe]])|1x [[Blood Donor Ring]] 32x [[Hemobomb]]|Recipe: [[Hemobomb]] x4 ×8 + [[Blood Donor Ring]] → Blood Donor Artifact|
## Usage
Accessory:
Blood Donor Artifact [[Rift Time]]: +15 [[Mana Regen]]: +10%
Accessory:
Blood Donor Artifact [[Health Regen]]: +3
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Blood Donor Artifact can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Blood Donor Artifact can be added to the player's [[Accessory Bag]] or kept in their inventory to grant the accessory's effects, which are slightly different when in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
## Trivia
- The Blood Donor Artifact and its predecessor, the [[Blood Donor Ring]], are the only items to give biological information in their lore.

Once obtained, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 4,000,000 [[Coins]].

# Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette
Aliases: Blegg, Blue Goblin Omelette, Blue Omelette
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools, Mining, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]
Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette.png May cause illness
Type: Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: May cause illness
HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) Requirement: Heart of the Mountain V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Goblin Omelette]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+15 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 20,840 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Blue Goblin Egg]] 96x [[Goblin Egg]] 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_OMELETTE_BLUE_CHEESE
Tooltip: Drill Part; Adds +1 Level to all of your unlocked Heart of the Mountain perks.; Put this item in the Upgrade Module slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; May cause illness; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 5.; RARE
The Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]] upgrade module that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. that adds +1 level to all unlocked [[Heart of the Mountain]] perks; it's able to bypass the level cap.
## Obtaining
The Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It requires 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Blue Goblin Egg|Blue Goblin Eggs]], 1 [[Goblin Omelette]], 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Sapphire Gemstone]], and 20 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] 1 Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette
  - 96 [[Blue Goblin Egg]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Sapphire Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
## Usage
The Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to grant the player +1 level to all unlocked [[Heart of the Mountain]] perks when the Drill is held. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
## Bugs
- At [[Heart of the Mountain|CotM (Core of the Mountain)]] level 2, using a [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette can bypass the cap of the [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]] ability by 10 [[Mining Fortune]], from 500 to 510 .
  At level 3 of [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]], each block broken grants +15 [[Mining Fortune]] . The check for the cap is run after the incrementation. In practice, this gives: +15 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → +30 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → […] → +480 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → +495 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → +510 [[Mining Fortune]] → STOP.

|Unknown Date|Removed extra line in lore.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Now requires Heart of the Mountain V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) instead of VI.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Now requires 96 Blue Goblin Eggs ([[Blue Goblin Egg]]) instead of 99 and 1 Flawless Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) and 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] instead of 1 Perfect Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]).|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Added Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette to [[Museum]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Blue Elephant Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Blue Elephant Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 5, 2020. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 500 purchases.

The Blue Elephant Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Elephant Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Blue Goblin Egg
Aliases: Blue Egg, Blue egg
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Crystal Hollows
[Blue Goblin Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dwarven Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_EGG_BLUE
Tooltip: What came first the goblin or the egg?; RARE
Blue Goblin Egg is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to forge the [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv89]|[[Creeperlobber]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
|[Lv65]|[[Goblin Flamethrower]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
|[Lv96]|[[Murderlover]]|1|10RNGesus Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.01%|
### Treasure Chests
Blue Goblin Eggs can be dropped from chests in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. The chests can either be found in naturally spawning structures like the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] and by [[King Yolkar]] or can be spawned by the player while mining [[Hard Stone|Hardstone]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
### [[Frozen Corpses]]
Blue Goblin Eggs can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Umber Tungsten|[[Blue Goblin Egg]] 1 Blue Goblin Egg|1.22% (1 in 81.75)|
|Vanguard|[[Blue Goblin Egg]] 1 Blue Goblin Egg|4.55% (1 in 21.97)|
|Vanguard|[[Blue Goblin Egg]] 2 Blue Goblin Egg|2.28% (1 in 43.94)|
## Usage
### Spawning Golden Goblins
Throwing a Blue Goblin Egg in [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] or [[Glacite Tunnels]] has a 10% (1 in 10 eggs) chance to spawn a [[Golden Goblin]], with a base 3.33% chance to be replaced with a [[Diamond Goblin]].
### [[The Forge|Forging]]
#### [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]
- 1 [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]
  - [[Blue Goblin Egg]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Blue Goblin Egg
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Sapphire Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
### Fuel
The Blue Goblin Egg can be used as a [[Machine Fuel]], adding 20,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bluertooth Ring]]|None|1x [[Bluetooth Ring]] [[Blue Goblin Egg]] 2x Blue Goblin Egg 16x [[Blue Shark Tooth]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Blue Ring]]|Recipe: [[Blue Ring]] x32 ×4 + Blue Goblin Egg ×2 + [[Blue Shark Tooth]] x8 ×2 + [[Bluetooth Ring]] → [[Bluertooth Ring]]|

The Bluetooth Ring is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Living Metal Heart IV (4) ([[Living Metal Heart]])|32x [[Living Metal Heart]]|Recipe: [[Living Metal Heart]] x4 ×8 → Bluetooth Ring|
## Usage
Accessory:
Bluetooth Ring [[Intelligence]]: -1 [[Speed]]: -2 [[Rift Time]]: +30s
[Effects]
[Monster melee attacks reduce 1 less ϕ Rift Time if there is at least another player within 50 blocks.]
Accessory:
Bluetooth Ring (Outside) ([[Bluetooth Ring]]) [[Intelligence]]: -1 [[Speed]]: -2
[Effects]
[Deal +1 Damage if there are at least 6 players on the island you're on.; But wait! There's more... Adds +3 Damage bonus to the Blazetekk™ Ham Radio.]
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Bluetooth Ring can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. When outside the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], the Bluetooth Ring grants different stats and abilities.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Subzero Inverter]] 1x [[Inferno Apex]] [[Bluetooth Ring]] 2x Bluetooth Ring 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×2 + [[Inferno Apex]] + Bluetooth Ring ×2 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Subzero Inverter]] + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x64 → [[Wisp Upgrade Stone]]|
|[[Bluertooth Ring]]|None|[[Bluetooth Ring]] 1x Bluetooth Ring 2x [[Blue Goblin Egg]] 16x [[Blue Shark Tooth]] 128x [[Blue Ring]]|Recipe: [[Blue Ring]] x32 ×4 + [[Blue Goblin Egg]] ×2 + [[Blue Shark Tooth]] x8 ×2 + Bluetooth Ring → [[Bluertooth Ring]]|

- This along with the [[Green Jerry Talisman|Jerry]] line of [[Accessories]] are the only [[Accessories]] that give negative [[Stats]].

# Bob-omb
Aliases: Bomb
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crystal Hollows
[Bob-omb]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Crystal Hollows]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Crystal Hollows Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BOB_OMB
Tooltip: Makes a huge explosion in the Crystal Hollows and Glacite Mineshafts, breaking nearby blocks and dropping 50% of the items.; RARE
Bob-omb is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
Bob-ombs are dropped from placing down all Crystals in [[Crystal Nucleus]] in [[Crystal Hollows]], in stacks of 20. It has a 3.255% chance to be dropped for each roll in the treasure bundle.
## Usage
Bob-ombs are consumable items. When one is used in the [[Crystal Hollows]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]], it throws a block of [[TNT]] in the direction the player is facing. After a short time, it explodes, destroying blocks in an 8 block radius regardless of their required [[Breaking Power]]. 50% of the blocks destroyed will be mined, dropping their resources.
## Trivia
- They are a reference to Bob-ombs from the Mario franchise.
- Blocks in a protected area cannot be bombed.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Bob-omb.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Bob-omb as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Bob-omb.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Bob-omb to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Oct 1, 2024|Decreased the weight from [[Crystal Nucleus]] loot from 12,000 to 10,000.|
|Oct 1, 2024|Increased amount gained from [[Crystal Nucleus]] loot from 16 to 20.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Bob-omb can now be sold to NPCs for 1,000 [[Coins]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Bob-omb as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

The Bolt Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Lightning Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Bolt Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|RARE]] and Combat Category
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bolt Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Lightning Elemental]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Lightning Elemental ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Strength]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Bolt Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

Bonzo's Mask is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Helmets|Helmet]].
## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
A Bonzo's Mask can be claimed as a one-time reward from [[Bonzo|Bonzo II (2)]], which is unlocked after killing [[Bonzo]] 50 times.
### Dungeon Reward Chests
A Bonzo's Mask can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor I]].

## Usage
Helmet:
Bonzo's Mask [[Health]]: +100 [[Defense]]: +100 [[Intelligence]]: +100
[Effects]
[Ability: Clownin' Around; Instead of dying, gain Damage immunity and +20 Strength for 3s and fully replenish your Health. The cooldown and Strength bonus you receive improve based on your Dungeoneering Skill (Dungeoneering) level. This ability only works while in The Catacombs (Catacombs)!; Cooldown: 360s]
Helmet:
[[Bonzo's Mask]] [[Health]]: +125 [[Defense]]: +100 [[Intelligence]]: +150
[Effects]
[Ability: Clownin' Around; Instead of dying, gain Damage immunity and +20 Strength for 3s and fully replenish your Health. The cooldown and Strength bonus you receive improve based on your Dungeoneering Skill (Dungeoneering) level. This ability only works while in The Catacombs (Catacombs)!; Cooldown: 360s]
The Bonzo's Mask's Clownin' Around ability protects the user from fatal damage while in the [[Catacombs]], instead granting +20 [[Strength]] for 3s and fully replenishing their health: this ability has a 360s cooldown. Depending on the user's [[Catacombs]] level, the cooldown will be reduced and the user will gain more [[Strength]]. The cooldown is reduced by 3.6s for each Catacombs level and at [[Catacombs]] level 50, its ability has a 180s cooldown.
## Upgrading
Bonzo's Mask can be converted into Bonzo's Mask using [[Bonzo Fragment|Bonzo Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +25 [[Health]]
- +50 [[Intelligence]]
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Bonzo's Mask]] 1x Bonzo's Mask 8x [[Bonzo Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Bonzo Fragment]] ×8 + Bonzo's Mask → [[Bonzo's Mask]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|5|5|
|✪✪|10|15|
|✪✪✪|25|40|
|✪✪✪✪|50; 10,000 [[Coins]]|90; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|100; 25,000 [[Coins]]|190; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jester Bonzo's Mask Skin]]|[[Jester Bonzo's Mask Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - September 9, 2023 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[Jester Bonzo's Mask Skin]]|[[Jester Bonzo's Mask Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - September 9, 2023 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Tips
- Bonzo's Mask and the [[Spirit Mask]] have separate cooldowns, allowing the player to swap helmets during one's cooldown.
## Trivia
A [[Bonzo's Mask]] Bonzo's Mask with [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration I]] is needed to obtain a [[Wet Napkin]].

## Usage
Sword:
Bonzo's Staff [[Damage]]: +160 [[Intelligence]]: +250
[Effects]
[Ability: Showtime RIGHT CLICK; Shoots balloons that create a large explosion on impact, dealing up to 1,000 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 90; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2]
Sword:
[[Bonzo's Staff]] [[Damage]]: +160 [[Intelligence]]: +300
[Effects]
[Ability: Showtime RIGHT CLICK; Shoots balloons that create a large explosion on impact, dealing up to 1,100 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 90; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2]
The Bonzo's Staff's Showtime ability shots slow-moving balloons at a rate of 4/s. These balloons explode on contact with blocks and mobs, which deals 1,000 damage to all mobs caught within 2 blocks of the explosion. [[Enchantments]] that do not affect abilities, such as [[Sharpness]], [[Life Steal]], [[Drain]], [[Cleave]], and [[Mana Steal]], as well as [[Ferocity]], do not work with the Showtime ability.
If the wielder is within range of the explosion, they will be launched upwards and backwards up to 1.7 blocks upwards and 8 blocks backwards.
## Upgrading
Bonzo's Staff can be converted into Bonzo's Staff using [[Bonzo Fragment|Bonzo Fragments]] to buff its stats, granting:
- +50 [[Intelligence]]
- Showtime now deals 1,100 damage
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Bonzo's Staff]] 1x Bonzo's Staff 8x [[Bonzo Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Bonzo Fragment]] ×8 + Bonzo's Staff → [[Bonzo's Staff]]|
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Wither Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|5|5|
|✪✪|10|15|
|✪✪✪|25|40|
|✪✪✪✪|50; 10,000 [[Coins]]|90; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|100; 25,000 [[Coins]]|190; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- The Bonzo's Staff, while not viable as a primary Mage weapon past the first few floors upon obtaining a weapon like a [[Spirit Sceptre]], is incredibly useful in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] as a mobility weapon as it can be used to:
  - Reach the Energy Crystals in [[Maxor]]'s phase
  - Quickly reach the pressure plates in [[Storm]]'s phase
  - Reach terminals and devices in [[Goldor]]'s phase
- It is imperative to upgrade the Bonzo's Staff with [[Bonzo Fragment|Bonzo Fragments]] due to their relatively cheap price on the Bazaar.
## Gallery
- In-hand model.
- Fragged in-hand model.

# Bottle of Jyrre
Aliases: Bottle Of Jyrre, Jyrre, Refined Bottle of Jyrre
Categories: Rare items, Legendary items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Aging Items, Events, Season of Jerry, Consumables, V0.7.5
[Bottle of Jyrre]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: NEW_BOTTLE_OF_JYRRE
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 30,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFINED_BOTTLE_OF_JYRRE
Tooltip: Consume to boost your  Intelligence for 60m.; Keep this item in your inventory to increase the bonus up to +300 Intelligence, at which point the item evolves!; Current Bonus: +0 Intelligence; RARE
Bottle of Jyrre is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. After reaching 300 bonus [[Intelligence]], it evolves into a Refined Bottle of Jyrre.
## Obtaining
Bottle of Jyrre has a 1.68% (1 in 59.64 gifts) chance to drop from opening [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]].
## Usage
### Aging Item
A Bottle of Jyrre gains 1 [[Intelligence]] every hour that the player is online with it in their inventory, taking 300 hours to reach the maximum buff of 300 [[Intelligence]]. This time can be sped up 2x by placing Bottle of Jyrre into [[Time Pocket]]. Furthermore, the [[Enchanted Clock]] and the Quiz Puzzle in the [[Catacombs]] increases the timer on all [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]] by 1 hour, which is doubled for items placed in the [[Time Pocket]].
Upon reaching 300 bonus [[Intelligence]], the Bottle of Jyrre evolves into a Refined Bottle of Jyrre with the following message.
EVOLUTION! Your Bottle of Jyrre has evolved!
Drinking a Refined Bottle of Jyrre grants 300 [[Intelligence]] for 1 hour, as well as permanent +1 [[Intelligence]]. This can be done 5 times.
Like the [[Lost Bottle of Jyrre]], the Bottle of Jyrre can be drank before it evolves.
Trying to drink a second Bottle of Jyrre will result in the following message:
You already have an active Bottle of Jyrre!
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Rosewater Flask]]|Wild Rose VI (6) ([[Wild Rose]])|[[Bottle of Jyrre]] 1x Bottle of Jyrre 32x [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|Recipe: [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x4 ×8 + Bottle of Jyrre → [[Rosewater Flask]]|
## Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Bottle of Jyrre
 template  = Template:Calculator/Bottle of Jyrre
 calcname  = Bottle of Jyrre Time calculator
 form      = jyrreTimeCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = starting|Starting Intelligence|0|int|0-300
 param     = ending|Desired Intelligence|300|int|0-300
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Trivia
- Jyrre is also an anagram of Jerry, which is the name's likely etymological origin.
- In a brewing stand, a Bottle of Jyrre acts much like a bottle of water, as it can be turned into an awkward potion using [[Nether Wart|nether warts]], which is not recommended.
- The NPC sell price does not increase until it becomes refined, unlike the [[Lost Bottle of Jyrre]].
- Drinking a Bottle of Jyrre will switch the player's hotbar slot one to the left, or if it's in the leftmost slot, one to the right, even if it fails due to one already being active.

# Bubblegum
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pet items
[Bubblegum]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Zog]]
Buy: 5,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_BUBBLEGUM
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Your pet fuses its power with placed Deployables to give them 2x duration.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; RARE PET ITEM
Bubblegum is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that doubles the duration of [[Deployables]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Bubblegum can be purchased from [[Zog]] in the [[Village]] for 5,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Bubblegum will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it increased duration of [[Deployables]] by 2x. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Bubblegum for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
The effect applies if the deployable is placed while a pet with Bubblegum equipped is summoned. If the player switches to a pet, the deployable reverts to its original duration.
## Bugs
- When swapping off a pet with Bubblegum equipped, the deployable's duration does not visually revert to the original duration.
## Trivia
- This is the most expensive item that [[Zog]] sells.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Bubblegum.|

The Bucket Of Dye is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that increases the chance of dropping [[Dyes]] by 1%.
## Obtaining
The Bucket of Dye is obtained by donating any custom [[Dyes|Dye]] to [[Vincent]]. The donated dye will appear in the item's lore and change the texture. Dyes from [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] or [[Taylor]]'s shop cannot be donated.
## Trivia
- On the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], it was possible to obtain a Bucket of Dye dyed with a [[Fire Sale]] dye.

# Bulky Stone
Aliases: Bulky
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Article stubs
[Bulky Stone]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Bulky
Applied To: [[Swords]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXV (25) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BULKY_STONE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Bulky reforge when combined with a Melee Weapon.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Bulky Stone is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the Bulky [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Weapons|melee weapon]], which grants [[Health]] and [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Bulky Stone can be obtained as a rare treasure after speaking with the [[Treasure Hunter]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Bulky Stone can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Bulky]] reforge, to grant [[Health]] and [[Defense]], which scale with the weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]]. Weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Bulky]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+4 HP ([[Health]]) +2 Def ([[Defense]])|+6 HP ([[Health]]) +3 Def ([[Defense]])|+9 HP ([[Health]]) +5 Def ([[Defense]])|+12 HP ([[Health]]) +8 Def ([[Defense]])|+15 HP ([[Health]]) +13 Def ([[Defense]])|+20 HP ([[Health]]) +21 Def ([[Defense]])|+25 HP ([[Health]]) +30 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- It is generally not recommended to put this on a weapon, even as a [[Tank]], due to the fact that the [[Health]] and [[Defense]] the reforge gives is incredibly low and the player would be better off putting other reforges on it.
  - It however can be used on items that the player would be only expected to hold when trying to survive, such as [[Wither Cloak Sword]], which already grants 250 [[Defense]].

The Bullfrog Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Berry Enjoyer]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Bullfrog Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: Frog Family
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Forest Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bullfrog Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Berry Enjoyer]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Berry Enjoyer ([[Attributes]]); Berries have a +10%–100% chance to grant you double points in Starlyn Contests ([[Starlyn Contest]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Bullfrog Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

A Busted Belt Buckle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained from [[Fishing]] while using a [[Junk Sinker]].
## Obtaining
A Busted Belt Buckle can be obtained as a GREAT JUNK CATCH by fishing with a [[Junk Sinker]] applied to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
## Usage
### Merchants
Busted Belt Buckles can be used to purchase various items from [[Junker Joel]].

[[Treasure Hook]]: 1x [[Bayou Water Orb]] 4x [[Old Leather Boot]] [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 32x Busted Belt Buckle 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Rusty Coin]]
### Quest
A Busted Belt Buckle can be given to [[Researcher Gilbert]] after reaching [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] to unlock access to the [[Lotus Atoll]].

|[Lv56]|[[Banshee]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Calcified Heart can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Calcified]] reforge, to grant [[Defense]] and [[Fishing Speed]], which scale with the armor's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece.
Additionally, it grants the Calcified Bonus, which grants 25 [[Defense]] against [[Sea Creatures]].

# Capsaicin Eyedrops
Aliases: Eyedrops
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Chili Pepper Collection
[Capsaicin Eyedrops]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Chili Pepper III (3) ([[Chili Pepper]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 4x [[Chili Pepper]] 20,480x (320 stacks (20,480 items total)) [[Carrot]] 2x [[Scribe Crux]] 2x [[Volt Crux]] 320x (5 stacks (320 items total)) [[Half-Eaten Carrot]] 1x [[Water Hydra Head]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 1 [[Water Hydra Head]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CAPSAICIN_EYEDROPS; CAPSAICIN_EYEDROPS_NO_CHARGES
Tooltip: Right-click on an Inferno minion with this item to boost its chances of creating unique drops from Hypergolic fuel by 30% for 24 hours.; RARE
Capsaicin Eyedrops is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to boost the chances of rare drops from [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] with [[Inferno Minion Fuel]] made from [[Hypergolic Gabagool]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Chili Pepper III (3) ([[Chili Pepper]])|1x [[Water Hydra Head]] 4x [[Chili Pepper]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Carrot]] 128x [[Exportable Carrots]]|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] ×4 + [[Exportable Carrots]] x64 ×2 + [[Enchanted Carrot]] x64 ×2 + [[Water Hydra Head]] → Capsaicin Eyedrops x3|
## Usage
The player can right-click on an [[Inferno Minion]] with Capsaicin Eyedrops, which will cause it to produce [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]], [[Inferno Apex|Inferno Apexes]], [[Reaper Pepper|Reaper Peppers]], and [[Gabagool the Fish]] 30% more often for 24 hours.
## Trivia
- Capsaicin is a neurotoxin and an active component of chili peppers in real-life, which causes a burning sensation for anything it comes in contact with.
  - This could be the reason why it makes [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] cry, which is displayed by particles dripping from their eyes.
- Capsaicin Eyedrops had two charges, however this was later removed on October 4, 2023.
## History

|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Capsaicin Eyedrops now have one charge and are stackable.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Revamped the Capsaicin Eyedrops crafting recipe.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Added Capsaicin Eyedrops to the [[Bazaar]].|

# Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set
Aliases: Full Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set, Vinyl Set
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Tools
[Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2,400x (37 stacks plus 32 (2,400 in total)) [[Oak Log]] 9x [[Carrot Zest]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CARROT_COLORED_VINYL_SET
Tooltip: Fill this with any 5 Pest Vinyls.; Once full, you may gift this to a Visitor to charm them.; Charmed Visitors can give better rewards next time they visit!; Vinyls: 0\ 5; Right-click to open!; RARE
A Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be used to charm [[Garden Visitors]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|9x [[Carrot Zest]] 15x [[Enchanted Oak Log]]|Recipe: [[Carrot Zest]] x3 ×3 + [[Enchanted Oak Log]] x3 ×5 → Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set|
## Usage
Up to any 5 [[Pest Vinyls]] can be placed in a Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set which can then be used to charm a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] which ensures that they will have better rewards the next time they visit. This consumes the Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set as well as the [[Pest Vinyls|Vinyls]].
Charming Visitors guarantees the following:
- 1 [[Visitors' Gratitude]]
- 1.5x [[Copper]]
It also gives 3x chance for the following items:
- [[Garden Chips|Hypercharge Chip]]
- [[Garden Chips|Quickdraw Chip]]
- [[Flowering Bouquet]]
- [[Overgrown Grass]]
- [[Green Bandana]]
- [[Fruit Bowl]]
- [[Copper Dye]]
- [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Dedication|Dedication IV]]) ( [[Librarian]] only)
- [[Wild Strawberry Dye]] ( [[Vincent]] only)
Additionally, gifting a Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set to [[Jacob]] or [[Spaceman]] awards a [[Farming Contest Display]] or [[Minion Skins|Astronaut Minion Skin]] respectively.
## Notes
- If a player charms a visitor using a Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set, they will bring better rewards upon their next visit regardless of whether their offer is accepted or declined during the visit they were charmed.
  - However, a visitor who is already offering a [[Visitors' Gratitude]] cannot be charmed, when already under the effect. The player must accept their offer and wait for them to visit again.
- There is no way to see which visitors are charmed in between the player charming them and the visitor returning.
  - It may be useful for players to write down the names of rarer visitors that they charm, in case they desire to use a [[Bachelor's Rose]] on that NPC in the future.
- When a visitor appears on the player's Garden charmed, rejecting their offer will remove the charm, without giving the rewards.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 31, 2026|0.24.3|Added Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added Carrot-Colored Vinyl Set.|

# Catalyst
Categories: Rare items, Minion fuels, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, End Stone Collection, Minion Modifiers, The End
[Catalyst]
Type: [[Minion Fuel]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): End Stone IX (9) ([[End Stone]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Hyper Catalyst]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1x [[Crystal Fragment]] 8x [[End Stone]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CATALYST
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; When in a minion, this increases the number of resources created by 3x for 3 hours!; RARE
The Catalyst is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Fuel]] item that multiplies minion resource generation by 3x for 3 hours. It triples the resources the minions would normally get from harvesting a block or a mob, therefore it doesn't actually have an impact on the time between actions. It is unlocked at [[End Stone|End Stone IX (9)]].
This can be upgraded to the [[Hyper Catalyst]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|End Stone IX (9) ([[End Stone]])|1x [[Crystal Fragment]] 8x [[End Stone]]|Recipe: [[End Stone]] ×8 + [[Crystal Fragment]] → Catalyst|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Catalysts increase the amount of resources generated by the minion by 3x for 3 hours. Catalyst can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hyper Catalyst]]|None|1x [[Hyper Catalyst Upgrade]] [[Catalyst]] 8x Catalyst|Recipe: Catalyst ×8 + [[Hyper Catalyst Upgrade]] → [[Hyper Catalyst]] x8|
## Trivia
- Using a Catalyst on a minion for the first time awards the [[Achievements|"At the speed of light" Achievement]].
- Catalysts used to be commonly used to craft beacons. However, in an update, they removed the ability to use a [[Day Crystal]], [[Night Crystal]], Catalyst, or [[Saving Grace]] to craft a [[Beacon Block|Beacon]].
- For some reason, the rarity of Catalysts is lower than that of the [[Crystal Fragment]] used to craft it.
- Spreading upgrades (e.g. [[Diamond Spreading]] and [[Sleepy Hollow]]) will apply to the extra drops gained from Catalyst's bonus.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Catalyst.|

Item ID: CHEESEBITE
Tooltip: A smell so aromatic that even rodents can't resist.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Cheesebite is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Cheesebite can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Creambloom]] and 4 [[Fermento]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Cheesebite needs water and has 10 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it retains water by 100% and decreases yield by -20%.
At Growth Stages 4 and 7, a [[Rat]] spawns beside the Cheesebite and eats it. While the Rat is alive, the Cheesebite stops growing. The [[Rat]] can be killed using a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] as if it was a regular [[Pests|Pest]].
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Cheesebites also drop 253 (3 stacks plus 61) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 253 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Cactus]], 1,067 (16 stacks plus 43) [[Sugar Cane]], and +6,400 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Cheesebite can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 80,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 80 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Cheesebite grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Cheesebites are used to create a [[Startlevine]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone V (5) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) [[Cheesebite]] 8x Cheesebite 8x [[Blastberry]] 16x [[Fleshtrap]]|Recipe: [[Fleshtrap]] x4 ×4 + [[Blastberry]] x4 ×2 + Cheesebite x4 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
|[[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]]|None|1x [[Jukebox]] [[Cheesebite]] 3x Cheesebite 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Botroot]]|Recipe: Cheesebite ×3 + [[Botroot]] x16 ×5 + [[Jukebox]] → [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Rat]] spawned by a Cheesebite does not provide loot, contribute to [[Bestiary]], nor increase the [[Pests|Pest]] counter on a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]]. It also cannot be caught with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to obtain a [[Rat Shard]].
  - They do, however, provide [[Farming]] experience and decrease [[The Garden|Visitor]] spawn time.
- Cheesebite is the only mutation that costs 1,000 [[Coins]] per [[Copper]] to analyze rather than 2,000 [[Coins]].
## Bugs
- When a large quantity of [[Rat|Rats]] spawned by a Cheesebite are killed, the message "You can only damage [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]]s with a Vacuum! Visit SkyMart to obtain one!" will appear despite having killed them with a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]].
- Using a [[Rosewater Flask]] on a [[The Garden|Garden Plot]] containing growing Cheesebites will not grow the Cheesebites past the next rat-spawn stage; a stage 3 Cheesebite will become a stage 4 Cheesebite instead of a stage 7 Cheesebite.
## Gallery
- Cheesebite in the Greenhouse Cheesebite in the [[Greenhouse]]

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Cheesebite.|

# Chloronite
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Chloronite]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHLORONITE
Tooltip: A plant that hardens into a shining green gemstone as it matures, ready to be mined.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Chloronite is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Chloronite can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 6 [[Coalroot]] and 2 [[Thornshade]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Chloronite needs water and has 10 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it grants immunity to negative effects and increases [[Farming]] experience by 20%.
Chloronite requires 1 [[Breaking Power]] to harvest.
Amount of Chloronite dropped is dependant on player's [[Mining Fortune]]:
|[[Mining Fortune]]|Amount of Chloronite drops|
|-|-|
|0-99 [[Mining Fortune]]|~ [[Chloronite]] 0–1 Chloronite|
|100-499 [[Mining Fortune]]|~ [[Chloronite]] 1–2 Chloronite|
|500-999 [[Mining Fortune]]|~ [[Chloronite]] 2–3 Chloronite|
|1000-1999 [[Mining Fortune]]|~ [[Chloronite]] 3–4 Chloronite|
|2000+ [[Mining Fortune]]|[[Chloronite]] 4 Chloronite|
For example, if the player has 1500 [[Mining Fortune]] , they will get 3 Chloronite, and a 50% chance to get another one. The amount of guaranteed drops that will be received can be viewed using the [[Plant Diagnostics Tool]].
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Chloronites also drop 128 (2 stacks) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 128 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 540 (8 stacks plus 28) [[Wild Rose]], 810 (12 stacks plus 42) [[Potato]], 945 (14 stacks plus 49) [[Carrot]], and +4,320 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Chloronite can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 40,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 20 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Chloronite grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Chloronites are used to create a [[Chorus Fruit]] and [[Glasscorn]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sundial]]|Sunflower VII (7) ([[Sunflower]])|[[Chloronite]] 5x Chloronite 16x [[Compacted Sunflower]]|Recipe: [[Compacted Sunflower]] x2 ×8 + Chloronite x5 → [[Sundial]]|

- Chloronite in the Greenhouse Chloronite in the [[Greenhouse]]

The Chuckwalla Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Subterranean Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Chuckwalla Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Chuckwalla]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Chuckwalla Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Chuckwalla Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Subterranean Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Subterranean Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] on [[Subterranean]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Chuckwalla Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Chuckwalla Shard has the following description:
- Chuckwallas cram themselves into the smallest of crevices to avoid predators, inflating their lungs to make themselves harder to dislodge.

# Chyme

[Chyme]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])

Item ID: CHYME
Tooltip: Consumable; Feeding this to your pets gives them a boost of 90,000 Exp!; Right-click on your summoned pet to feed it!; RARE
Chyme is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that gives 90,000 Pet XP.
## Obtaining
Chyme can be obtained by bringing a player without Armor to [[Aranya]] and letting her eat them. It can only be obtained once every seven days.
Chyme can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
Right-Clicking with a Chyme in hand will add 90,000 Pet XP to the player's active Pet. Chyme doesn't have any limits, unlike the [[Candy (disambiguation)|Carrot Candies]].

Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Buzzing reforge when combined with a Vacuum.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Farming Fortune; Buzzing Bonus This item deals x2 Damage towards  Pests.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 15.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Clipped Wings is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Buzzing]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Vacuums]]. It grants [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Mosquito]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%* ([[Pests]])|
## Usage
### Reforging
Clipped Wings can be combined with a [[Vacuums|Vacuum]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Buzzing]] reforge, to grant [[Farming Fortune]], which scales with the Vacuum's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]]. A Vacuum that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Buzzing]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Buzzing Bonus, which makes the item deal ×2 [[Damage]] against [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pests]].

- If one is able to oneshot [[Pests]], with the help of [[Attributes|Pest Ruler]], 5 [[Bookworm's Favorite Book]] and [Lv100] [[Hedgehog Pet]] (which works unless [[Derpy]] is elected [[Mayor Election|Mayor]]), [[Reforging|Beady]] is preferable over Buzzing, due to its 100 [[Farming Fortune]] against Pests.

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|160x [[Compost]]|Recipe: [[Compost]] x32 ×7 → Compost Bundle|
## Usage
Compost Bundle are used to expand the number of plots in [[The Garden]] from the 12th to 24th plots.
[[Compost Bundle]] 1 Compost Bundle and 10,000,000 [[Coins]] are used to upgrade the [[Slug Pet]] from [[Rarity|EPIC]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] at [[Kat]].
[[Compost Bundle]] 10 Compost Bundle and 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Ethereal Vine]] are used to purchase a second [[Greenhouse]] plot, [[Compost Bundle]] 20 Compost Bundle and 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Ethereal Vine]] are used to purchase a third [[Greenhouse]] plot.

# Control Switch
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Crystal Hollows
[Control Switch]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Crystal Hollows Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CONTROL_SWITCH
Tooltip: A key component needed by the Professor to repair Automaton Prime in the Lost Precursor City!; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Control Switch is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item and one of the items required to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv71]|[[Automaton]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv67]|[[Automaton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Treasure Chests
A Control Switch can also be found as an drop from treasure chests within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. It has a 0.34% chance to roll as a drop in common loot chests, and a 0.29% chance to roll as a drop in rare loot chests.
## Usage
### Sapphire Gemstone Crystal
Bringing a Control Switch and the 5 other [[Automaton]] components to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]] rewards the player with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Precursor Apparatus]]|None|1x [[Electron Transmitter]] 1x [[Superlite Motor]] 1x [[FTX 3070]] 1x [[Robotron Reflector]] [[Control Switch]] 1x Control Switch 1x [[Synthetic Heart]]|Recipe: Control Switch + [[Electron Transmitter]] + [[FTX 3070]] + [[Robotron Reflector]] + [[Superlite Motor]] + [[Synthetic Heart]] → [[Precursor Apparatus]]|

The Cool Rock Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on September 23, 2020. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 600 purchases.

Creeper Pants are [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Leggings]] unlocked at [[Gunpowder|Gunpowder VIII (8)]]. It is part of the secret [[Monster Hunter Armor]] set.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gunpowder VIII (8) ([[Gunpowder]])|224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] x32 ×7 → Creeper Pants|
## Usage
Leggings:
Creeper Pants [[Health]]: +200 [[Defense]]: +65
[Effects]
[Ability: Detonate; Causes an explosion when dropping below 20% HP, damaging and knocking back all monsters around you.; Cooldown: 60s]
Creeper Pants' Detonate ability causes the wearer to cause an explosion that deals knockback and damage to mobs in a radius upon dropping to under 20% [[Health]]. This ability has a cooldown of 60s.
## Trivia
- The Creeper Pants are another reference to the Hypixel minigame [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]]. In Mega Walls, the Creeper class's main skill is to activate an explosion that causes massive AOE damage (Detonate). Also, the Creeper's signature kit item is it's diamond leggings.
- The ability can affect other players, but the knockback other players take from it is very low.

The Crocodile Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Pure Reptile]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Crocodile Shard can be purchased from [[Kiara]] for 300,000 [[Coins]]. The player can purchase up to 6 per day, or up to 7 with her [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] unlocked.
### Fusion
A Crocodile Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Crocodile Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Pure Reptile]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Pure Reptile ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +2%–20% chance to double shards from a Reptile Fusion.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Crocodile Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Crocodile Shard has the following description:
- Crodociles lurk silently in the murky waters, their eyes and nostrils barely visible above the surface as they wait for unsuspecting prey.
