# Crooked Artifact
Categories: Rare items, Accessories, Artifact, Dark Auction
[Crooked Artifact]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Shady Ring]]
Next →: [[Seal of the Family]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CROOKED_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Get 2% off on most shops.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Crooked Artifact is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that gives its user 2% off on most shops.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Crooked Artifact can be purchased from [[Lucius]] for 2,000,000 [[Coins]] and a [[Shady Ring]] after buying 3 items from the [[Dark Auction]].
If the player loses the item, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] in the [[Gold Mine]] for 2,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Crooked Artifact
[Effects]
[Get 2% off on most shops.]
### Shop Purchase
The Crooked Artifact can be upgraded to the [[Seal of the Family]] for 10,000,000 [[Coins]] after buying 7 [[Dark Auction]] items.
## Trivia
- The price-reducing effect of the Crooked Artifact works with most NPC shops in the game.
  - Notably it doesn't work for NPCs that provide a service, rather than selling items such as: [[Vanessa]], [[Maddox the Slayer]], [[Kat]], [[Fann]], [[Blacksmith]], [[Geo]], [[Jotraeline Greatforge]], [[Treasure Hunter]], analyzing mutations at [[Jake]], [[Teddy]] and [[Freddy]], [[Jacobus]], [[Zarina]] and [[Roddy]].
  - Despite being accessible by, owned by or related to NPCs, it doesn't affect [[Auction House]], [[Bazaar]], [[Dark Auction]], [[Shen's Auction]], the [[Regalia Room]], recipes that cost [[Coins]] at [[The Forge|Forge]], opening [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] or [[Richard|Stonks Auction]].
    - On the other hand, it does affect [[The Garden|Skymart]], which isn't accessed via any NPCs.
  - Still, there are certain shops that aren't affected; known exceptions include: [[Bea]], [[Fann]], the [[Golden Dragon]], [[Grumblefoot]], the [[Jade Dragon]], [[Ludleth]], [[Oringo]], [[Rosetta]], [[Seymour]], [[Jax]], [[Odger]] and [[Researcher Ribery]]
    - Oddly, Certain shops are affected only partially. It affects all items in [[Brynmor]]'s shop except [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Packrat Shard]]. Several items from [[Rusty]]'s shop aren't affected either.
  - It even works with [[Centauri]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], but only for costs above 1 [[Coins|Coin]], such as when trading a stack of items.
- Despite not being part of the item's description, it only affect [[Coins]] prices, and won't affect other currencies or the amounts of items needed to purchase something.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 3, 2019|Added Crooked Artifact.|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Crooked Artifact was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Fixed Crooked Artifact not keeping recombobulation ([[Recombobulator 3000]]) when upgraded to [[Seal of the Family]].|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed purchase requirement from 10 [[Dark Auction]] purchases to 3.|

# Dark Cacao Truffle
Aliases: Refined Dark Cacao Truffle
Categories: Rare items, Legendary items, Items, Aging Items, Consumables
[Dark Cacao Truffle]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DARK_CACAO_TRUFFLE
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFINED_DARK_CACAO_TRUFFLE
Tooltip: Consume to boost your  Cocoa Beans Fortune for 60m.; Keep this item in your inventory to increase the bonus up to +30 Cocoa Beans Fortune, at which point the item evolves!; Current Bonus: +0 Cocoa Beans Fortune; RARE
Dark Cacao Truffle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that grants [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]]. After reaching 30 bonus [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]], it evolves into a Refined Dark Cacao Truffle.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Up to 3 Dark Cacao Truffle per [[Time Systems|SkyBlock year]] can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 2,500,000,000 [[Chocolate]] each upon reaching Chocolate Factory IV.
## Usage
Upon reaching 30 bonus [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]], the Dark Cacao Truffle evolves into a Refined Dark Cacao Truffle with the following message:
EVOLUTION! Your Dark Cacao Truffle has evolved!
Eating a Refined Dark Cacao Truffle grants 30 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]], for 60 minutes. It also grants a permanent 1 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]]. The permanent buff can only be applied 5 times.
It gains 0.1 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]] every hour, taking 300 hours to reach the maximum buff of 30 [[Crop Fortune|Cocoa Beans Fortune]]. This time can be sped up 2x by placing Dark Cacao Truffle into [[Time Pocket]]. Furthermore, the [[Enchanted Clock]] and the Quiz Puzzle in the [[Catacombs]] increases the timer on all [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]] by 1 hour, which is doubled for items placed in the [[Time Pocket]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming Dark Cacao Truffle|You consumed a Dark Cacao Truffle and gained +X Cocoa Beans Fortune for 60m!|
## History

|Aug 25, 2026|Changed purchase limit from 2 to 3 per SkyBlock year.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|May 28, 2024|0.20.2|Added purchase limit of 2 per SkyBlock year.|
|Jul 9, 2024|0.20.4|Fixed Dark Cacao Truffles effect lasting longer than intended.|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Changed Global Fortune Bonuses to Cocoa Beans Fortune ([[Crop Fortune]]).|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|Changed purchase limit from 2 to 3 per SkyBlock year.|

Dark Goggles are a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeon Items|Dungeon Helmet]] available to buy from [[Ophelia]] for 80,000 [[Coins]] and can be upgraded with [[Wither Essence]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Dark Goggles can be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for 80,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Dark Goggles [[Intelligence]]: +150 [[Ability Damage]]: +25
### Shop Purchase
Dark Goggles can be upgraded into [[Shadow Goggles]] at [[Ophelia]].
## Essence Crafting

|✪✪|20|30|

## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|

|[[Corrupt Wither Goggles Helmet Skin]]|[[Corrupt Wither Goggles Helmet Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 30, 2024 - 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|Features!; ▶ This skin is swappable between 10 evil variants.; ▶ Keeps track of the total [[Dungeon Score]] earned while wearing this skin.|
|(same as above)|[[Corrupt Wither Goggles Helmet Skin]]|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - November 30, 2024 - 1,800 [[SkyBlock Gems]]||
## Tips
- [[Shadow Goggles]] are an upgraded version of Dark Goggles and require clearing [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] to purchase.
- This item is really good for the [[Mage]] class. However, if you are struggling to survive, a [[Spirit Mask]], [[Bonzo's Mask]] or [[Adaptive Armor|Adaptive Helmet]] may be a better option.
- This helmet gives a lot of intelligence when 5-starred with a high [[Catacombs]] level.
- Two Good [[Reforge Stones]] for this item are: [[Reforging|Necrotic]] (given by [[Necromancer's Brooch]], grants large quantities of [[Intelligence]]) and [[Reforging|Giant]] (given by [[Giant Tooth]] for [[Health]])

## Obtaining
### Boss Collection
Dark Orb can be claimed once as a reward from [[Livid|Livid I (1)]], which is unlocked after killing [[Livid]] 50 times.

# Dead Cat Food
Aliases: Cat Food, Cat food
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Dead Cat Food]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEAD_CAT_FOOD
Tooltip: Consumed on use; This pet item can be used on Montezuma. It may have a different effect outside of the Rift.; Grants +30 Rift Time and +5 Mana Regen.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; X Rift-Transferable X RARE PET ITEM
Dead Cat Food is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
Dead Cat Food can be bid on at [[Barrier Street]]. The top 60 bidders win the item.
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Montezuma Pet|Montezuma]] whilst holding a Dead Cat Food will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +30 [[Rift Time]] and +5 [[Mana Regen]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Dead Cat Food for 7,500 [[Motes]] and remove the perks it gave.
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Dead Cat Food can also be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], Dead Cat Food will be converted into [[Tasty Cat Food]].
## Trivia
- It is the only item that creates another when transferred outside of the [[Rift Dimension]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Dead Cat Food.|

|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Dead Cat Food.|

# Deepterror Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[Deepterror Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 64x [[Mite Gel]] 1x [[Ritual Residue]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEEPTERROR_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain +210 Health and +40 Defense in The End and the Crimson Isle.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
Deepterror Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Ritual Residue]] 64x [[Mite Gel]]|Recipe: [[Mite Gel]] x16 ×4 + [[Ritual Residue]] → Deepterror Mixin|
## Usage
### Brewing
A Deepterror Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +210 [[Health]] and +40 [[Defense]] when on [[The End]] and [[Crimson Isle]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Deepterror Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Trivia
- The Deepterror Mixin was the first [[Mixins|Mixin]] with an associated crafting recipe.
  - The [[Celestial Mason Jar]], [[Melon Juice Mixin]], and [[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]] were added later.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added Deepterror Mixin.|
Linked from: [[Health]], [[Mite Gel]], [[Mixins]], [[Ritual Residue]]

# Detective Scanner
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Detective Scanner]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DETECTIVE_SCANNER
Tooltip: Ability: Detect! RIGHT CLICK Investigate a clue or abnormality.; Mana Cost: 10 Cooldown: 4s; RARE
The Detective Scanner is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Detective Scanner can be claimed from [[Detective Amog]].
## Usage
Item:
Detective Scanner
[Effects]
[Ability: Detect! RIGHT CLICK; Invest a clue or abnormality.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 10; Cooldown: 4s]
The player can right-click on any green particles surrounding objects found in the [[Murder House]] to progress in [[Detective Amog]]'s reverse murder quest. The following objects in the [[Murder House]] can be investigated:
|Object|Dialogue when Investigated|
|-|-|
|Flower Pot|- Flower Pot - This amazing bouquet has been made with love, no doubt about that.|
|Blood|- Blood - This blood stain has been looked at before. That definitely spooked them quite a lot, perhaps they ran away. But where did they run...|
|Ring|- Ring - This is clearly an engagement ring. However, there is a curve on the front where there should be a jewel. This is incomplete.|
|Fridge|- Fridge - The fridge is empty. But what are these? BlockDash receipts?? Hundreds of them!|
|Tall Painting|- Tall Painting - It looks like an abstract painting of our victim. They seem to be wearing a red-ish top and blue jeans.|
|Window|- Window - The attacker might've escaped through this window! Or one of the other windows! Or through the door! Or the chimney!!!|
|Large Painting|- Large Painting - A picture of the future wedding. This looks like the bride. The husband is missing from the picture of course, he will be dead by then... Unfortunately the picture is too pixelated to recognize the wife.|
|Letter|- Letter - A love letter! It appears that they are engaged. What a tragedy... They might have gone to warn their love of their incoming murder.|
Examining all eight objects will allow the player to speak to [[Detective Amog]] to continue the quest. The following message will be sent as well:
You've examined all the clues!
Talk to the detective.
Later in the quest, the player is tasked with scanning the room again:
|Object|Dialogue when Investigated|
|-|-|
|Large Painting|- Large Painting - The wedding dress in that picture is fresh, it was just made. You can tell by the pixels. Talk to the detective again.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIAMOND_ATOM
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Perfect reforge when combined with Armor.; This miniscule stone is the dream of every blacksmith.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Defense; Perfect Bonus Grants +2%  Defense.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 22.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Diamond Atom is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Perfect]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv63-673]|[[Angry Archaeologist]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Diamond Atom can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Perfect]] reforge, to grant [[Defense]] which scales with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXII (22)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Perfect]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Perfect Bonus, which increases the player's [[Defense]] by 2%.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+25 Def ([[Defense]])|+35 Def ([[Defense]])|+50 Def ([[Defense]])|+65 Def ([[Defense]])|+80 Def ([[Defense]])|+110 Def ([[Defense]])|+140 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|Perfect Bonus; Grants +2% Defense.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Before 0.27.2, the Diamond Atom used a recolored version of the texture used by the [[Midas Jewel]] and [[Lapis Crystal]].
- Applying the [[Reforging|Perfect]] reforge to a piece of [[Perfect Armor]] changes the duplicate prefix from Perfect to Absolutely Perfect. For example, Perfect Perfect Boots becomes Absolutely Perfect Boots.

# Diamond Spreading
Categories: Rare items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Diamond Collection, Minion Modifiers, Items, Upgrades
[Diamond Spreading]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Diamond VI (6) ([[Diamond]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 640 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Diamond]] 8x [[Vines]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: [[Vines]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIAMOND_SPREADING
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. Place it in any minion and it will occasionally generate Diamonds!; RARE
Diamond Spreading is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]], unlocked at [[Diamond|Diamond VI (6)]]. Any [[Minions|Minion]] with this upgrade will occasionally collect [[Diamond]], alongside the regular item it collects.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Diamond VI (6) ([[Diamond]])|1x [[Enchanted Diamond]] 8x [[Vines]]|Recipe: [[Vines]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Diamond]] → Diamond Spreading|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Diamond Spreading can be placed in all [[Minions]]. For every item that the minion harvests, it has a 0.1 (1 in 10) chance to generate a [[Diamond]]. Minions that collect multiple items at once, such as [[Snow Minion|Snow Minions]], can make multiple diamonds at once. Diamond Spreading does not affect the minion's speed or other resources generated by the minion.
This can also be used to provide a small amount of additional [[Mining]] experience from the minion.
## Trivia
- Diamond Spreading will give on average of 1 [[Diamond]] for every 10 items harvested.
  - As such, it is recommended to put it on minions that collect a large amount of items per collection event.
- 1 Diamond Spreading is allowed per minion.
- Diamond Spreading used to prevent a minion from obtaining its natural resource upon an action.
- Diamond Spreading is compatible with [[Diamond Minion|Diamond Minions]], giving them effectively a 10% increase in [[Diamond]] production efficiency.
- An upgrade for Diamond Spreading was considered to be added with the [[Essence Shops|Diamond Essence Shop]], but was ultimately scrapped.
- Crafting a Diamond Spreading and selling on [[Bazaar]] can be used to price [[Vines]] at per.
## History

|Feb 16, 2021|0.11.1|Changed rarity from UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) to RARE ([[Rarity]]).|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Diamond Spreading to the [[Bazaar]].|

# Diana's Bookshelf
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Diana's Bookshelf]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIANAS_BOOKSHELF
Tooltip: Consumable; While in your inventory, counts the number of Chimera I books that you obtain from the Mythological Ritual event.; If you obtain 6 books you can consume this item to permanently gain:; +1 Magic Find +1 Tracking +1☯ Taming Wisdom; Books obtained: 0\ 6; You may only gain this bonus once!; RARE
Diana's Bookshelf is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
Diana's Bookshelf can be purchased from the [[Regalia Room]] for 75,000,000 [[Coins]]. As it is a rotating item, it may not be available every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
Diana's Bookshelf can also be obtained from [[Raffle of the Century]] event.
## Usage
The player can keep Diana's Bookshelf in their inventory to track how many [[Chimera|Chimera I]] books they drop from [[Minos Inquisitor|Minos Inquisitors]].
If the player has multiple uncompleted Diana's Bookshelves in their inventory, only the one with the highest count of books, or in case of a tie, the first one will be incremented.
Finding 6 [[Chimera|Chimera I]] books will allow the player to consume the item and gain a permanent stat bonus of the following:
- +1 [[Magic Find]]
- +1 [[Tracking]]
- +1 [[Taming Wisdom]]
The player may only gain the bonus once.
## Game Message
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|As you slot the last book into Diana's Bookshelf, it buckles under the weight and falls apart. You feel smarter already. (You have unlocked the bonus for Diana's Bookshelf)|
## History

|Feb 14, 2025|Added Diana's Bookshelf to the [[Regalia Room]].|
|Feb 14, 2025|Diana's Bookshelf now becomes consumable instead of resetting upon completion.|
|Feb 14, 2025|Fixed being able to increment multiple Diana's Bookshelves at once.|
|Nov 4, 2025|0.23.6|Diana's Bookshelf now gives +1 [[Tracking]] instead of +1 [[Pet Luck]].|

# Do-not-eat-shroom
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Do-not-eat-shroom]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DO_NOT_EAT_SHROOM
Tooltip: It's clearly labeled, but who can resist? Eat at your own risk - things get strange when this one's consumed.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Do-not-eat-shroom is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Do-not-eat-shroom can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Veilshroom]] and 4 [[Scourroot]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Do-not-eat-shroom needs water and has 8 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases yield by 30% and reduces water retain by -30%.
Harvesting a Do-not-eat-shroom grants Blindness III for 3 seconds and Nausea III for 5 seconds.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Do-not-eat-shrooms also drop 200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 200 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 630 (9 stacks plus 54) [[Potato]], 735 (11 stacks plus 31) [[Carrot]], and +3,360 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Do-not-eat-shroom can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 240,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 120 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Do-not-eat-shroom grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Do-not-eat-shrooms are used to create a [[Puffercloud]].
### Pet Upgrades
[[Do-not-eat-shroom]] 8 Do-not-eat-shroom are needed to upgrade a [[Crow Pet]] from [[Rarity|EPIC]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].
## Gallery
- Do-not-eat-shroom in the Greenhouse Do-not-eat-shroom in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History

# Doubloon of the Family
Aliases: Doubloon Of The Family
Categories: Rare items, Items
[Doubloon of the Family]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FAMILY_DOUBLOON
Tooltip: Worth 100,000 coins in the outside world.; A precious trinket from a powerful member of The Family who doesn't really need it anymore....; # Rift-Transferable # RARE
The Doubloon of the Family is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
A Doubloon of the Family can be claimed from [[Inverted Sirius]] in the [[Rift Dimension]] once the player has given them a [[Stability Elixir]].
## Usage
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Doubloon of the Family can also be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Doubloon of the Family can be sold to any [[Shops|Merchant]]. Doing so will reward the player with 100,000 [[Coins]].

The Draconic Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Essence of Dragons]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Draconic Shard is guranteed to drop when killing [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]] in the [[Dragon's Nest]] of [[The End]] with at least 125 [[Ender Dragon|Loot Quality]].
### Fusion
A Draconic Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Draconic Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Essence of Dragons]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Essence of Dragons ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% more [[Dragon Essence]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Dragon Claw
Aliases: Fabled
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Crafting ingredients, Mob Drops
[Dragon Claw]
Type: Reforge Stone
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Fabled
Applied To: [[Swords]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXII (22) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DRAGON_CLAW
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Fabled reforge when combined with a Melee Weapon.; Dragons rarely use their claws.; They should.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Crit Damage; Fabled Bonus  Crits have a chance to deal up to +15% more  Damage.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 22.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Dragons rarely use their claws. They should.
Dragon Claw is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]], which can apply the [[Reforging|Fabled]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Weapons|melee weapon]], which grants [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]].
## Obtaining
### [[Ender Dragon]]
Dragon Claw has 2% × number of [[Summoning Eye]] placed chance to be dropped after killing an [[Ender Dragon]], with over 446 [[Ender Dragon|Dragon Weight]].
### [[Draconic Altar]]
Dragon Claw has a 2.35% chance to be obtained as a bonus item after sacrificing [[Aspect of the Dragons]].
- There is a 45% chance for a bonus item to be rolled. This can be increased by up to 10% with the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Endermite Pet]]'s Sacrificer ability.
## Usage
### Reforging
Dragon Claw can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Fabled]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], and [[Crit Damage]], which scale with the weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXII (22)]]. A weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Fabled]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Fabled Bonus, which grants a chance for critical hits to deal up to 15% more damage.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+15 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +30 Str ([[Strength]])|+20 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +35 Str ([[Strength]])|+25 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +40 Str ([[Strength]])|+32 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +50 Str ([[Strength]])|+40 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +60 Str ([[Strength]])|+50 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +75 Str ([[Strength]])|+65 CD ([[Crit Damage]]) +90 Str ([[Strength]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|Fabled Bonus; Crits have a chance to deal up to 15% more damage.|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl IV (4) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] [[Dragon Claw]] 4x Dragon Claw 8x [[Crystal Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Crystal Fragment]] x2 ×4 + Dragon Claw ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
## Tips
- While [[Reforging|Fabled]] is the meta for Damage per second, players with lower stats can deal more damage with [[Reforging|Withered]], since the former grants a percentage boost to damage, while the latter grants a lot of stats.
  - When using [[Dark Claymore]] as Left-Click Mage, [[Reforging|Withered]] is always preferred. However when using [[Hyperion]] as Left-Click Mage, [[Reforging|Fabled]] is preferred for its [[Crit Damage]], as Hyperion gives none on its own.
## Trivia
- [[Dragon Claw]] Dragon Claw, as well as [[Dragon Scale]] and [[Dragon Horn]] are most likely references to the game The Legend of Zelda: Breath of the Wild, in which Link can shoot the three dragons Dinraal, Naydra and Farosh for item materials. Depending on his targeting of the claw, scale, or horn, they will receive that specific item. Likewise, in Hypixel SkyBlock, the three materials are ranked from worst to best: Scale, Claw, and Horn.
## Bugs
- Additional hits from [[Attack Speed]] that received the Fabled Bonus may occasionally not include [[Crit Damage]] in their damage calculation.

Hard to tell, really.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage  Attack Speed  Intelligence  Speed; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 20.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Dragon Scale is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Spiked]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Speed]], [[Strength]], [[Intelligence]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]], and [[Attack Speed]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Dragon Scale has a 30% base chance to drop from a [[Ender Dragon|Young Dragon]] after reaching at least 296 [[Ender Dragon|Loot Quality]].
It also has a 0.1% chance to drop from a Superior [[Lost Adventurer]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv103-866]|Lost Adventurer (Superior) ([[Lost Adventurer]])|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
|[Lv325]|Young Dragon ([[Ender Dragon]])|1||30%* ([[Ender Dragon]])|
## Usage
### Reforging
The Dragon Scale can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Spiked]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Speed]], [[Strength]], [[Intelligence]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]], and [[Attack Speed]] which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XX (20)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Spiked]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

## Trivia
- The [[Dragon Scale]] Dragon Scale as well as [[Dragon Claw]] and [[Dragon Horn]] are most likely references to the Nintendo Switch game The Legend of Zelda: Breath of the Wild released in 2017, in which Link can shoot the three dragons Dinraal, Naydra and Farosh for item materials. Depending on his targeting of the claw, scale, or horn, he will receive that specific item. Likewise, in Hypixel SkyBlock, the three materials are ranked from worst to best: Scale, Claw, and Horn.
- The [[Dragon Horn]]'s [[Reforging|Renowned]] reforge grants the same base stat values as [[Reforging|Spiked]], but additionally increases all [[Stats|Combat Stats]] and [[Magic Find]] by 1%.
## Notes
- The Two-Headed Strike perk from the [[Essence Shops|Dragon Essence Shop]] increases the [[Attack Speed]] granted by [[Reforging|Spiked]] by 2 per tier of the perk, up to 10 [[Attack Speed]].

The Dreadwing Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Essence of the Forest]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Dreadwing Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Dreadwing]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Huntraps
A Dreadwing Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] (On Land).
### Fusion
A Dreadwing Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Phanpyre Shard]] or [[Phanflare Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher

# Dungeon Chest Key
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Catacombs, Dungeons, Tools, Other Tools, Dungeon Items
[Dungeon Chest Key]
Type: Dungeon item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dungeon Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUNGEON_CHEST_KEY
Tooltip: Allows you to open an additional treasure chest at the end of a dungeon!; RARE DUNGEON ITEM
The Dungeon Chest Key is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] item that can be found from secrets. It allows a second [[Dungeon Reward Chest]] to be opened when the Dungeon is completed.
## Obtaining
Dungeon Chest Keys can be found from [[Dungeon Secrets|secrets]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor 4]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and above. The drop chance is 0.6%.
Dungeon Chest Key can also be obtained as a MINOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]], or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
Dungeon Chest Keys allow the player to open a second [[Dungeon Reward Chest|dungeon post-boss chest]] after a dungeons run. When the player holds a dungeon chest key in their inventory, they can view the contents and the GUI of other dungeon chests when right-clicking on them. The chest is not opened until the open button within the GUI menu is clicked. Only one dungeon chest key may be used per run. When the 2nd chest is opened, the key is consumed.
## Trivia
- For a short time after [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor 4's]] release, the Dungeon Chest Key was able to be [[Reforging|reforged]] like a normal [[Accessories|accessory]] which gave a stat bonus. However, this was unintentional and it was patched shortly after. The item's reforge is not removable, as the feature was removed, though the [[Accessories|accessory]] [[Reforging|reforge]] is affected by [[Dungeon Items|dungeon scaling]], giving it a bigger stat boost inside [[Dungeons]].
  - The [[Reforging|reforged]] Dungeon Chest Key is extraordinarily rare. Only 5 such items are known to exist, though there may be more unknown ones.
  - In another patch these items have become blacklisted. The only known one to exist was on the island of the player 15h in an item frame; however, 15h was later wiped, deleting it.

## Obtaining
Dwarven O's Block Bran has a 10% chance to drop when killing [[Scatha|Scathas]].
## Usage
Dwarven O's Block Bran can be consumed up to 5 times. Each Dwarven O's Block Bran eaten permanently increases the player's [[Block Fortune]] by +2 , up to +10 [[Block Fortune]].

|Attempting to eat more than 5|I think you should leave some for the rest of the players.|

- The messages for eating Dwarven O's Block Bran are each references.
  - Both "rock and stone" and "for Karl!" reference Deep Rock Galactic.
  - "They're magically delicious!" is the tagline for Lucky Charms.
  - "They're grrreat!!" is the tagline for Frosted Flakes.

# Dwarven Super Compactor
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Mithril Collection, Minion Modifiers, Dwarven Mines
[Dwarven Super Compactor]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Mithril VI (6) ([[Mithril]])
Function: Smelts and Compacts items produced by minions into their enchanted form.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 8,960x (140 stacks (8,960 items total)) [[Mithril]] 71,744x (1,121 stacks (71,744 items total)) [[Cobblestone]] 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]]) 1x [[Coal]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DWARVEN_COMPACTOR
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade.; Automatically smelts materials produced by minions before compacting them into their enchanted form.; RARE
A Dwarven Super Compactor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion upgrade]]. It is unlocked at [[Mithril|Mithril VI (6)]].
While placed in a [[Minions|Minion]] a Dwarven Super Compactor smelts produced items and compacts them, acting as a combination of both an [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]]. The player does not need the recipe of the compacted items unlocked.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mithril VI (6) ([[Mithril]])|1x [[Super Compactor 3000]] 1x [[Auto Smelter]] 56x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mithril]] x8 ×7 + [[Auto Smelter]] + [[Super Compactor 3000]] → Dwarven Super Compactor|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
A Dwarven Super Compactor can be placed in all [[Minions]]. It automatically smelts the materials harvested by the Minion and compacts all the items in the Minion's storage: this also applies to secondary-tier compacted items, such as [[Enchanted Iron Block|Enchanted Iron Blocks]] or [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop|Enchanted Cooked Porkchops]]. A Dwarven Super Compactor smelts the same items as [[Auto Smelter]].
The player does not need to have the recipe for any compacted item unlocked for a Dwarven Super Compactor to produce one.
## Tips
- If placed in a [[Cobblestone Minion]], the Dwarven Super Compactor will smelt the [[Cobblestone]] into [[Stone]], but will not compact it, as there is no enchanted form of [[Stone]]. This same concept applies to [[Sand Minion|Sand Minions]] ( [[Glass]] from [[Sand]]) and [[Clay Minion|Clay Minions]] ( [[Bricks]] from [[Clay Ball|Clay Balls]]). For these three minions, it's better to use a [[Super Compactor 3000]].
- For [[Iron Minion|Iron]] and [[Gold Minion]], smelting is needed before compacting, and using Dwarven Super Compactor instead of [[Super Compactor 3000]] and [[Auto Smelter]] saves one upgrade slot.
## Trivia
- If a minion also has a regular [[Compactor]] and the blocks it makes cannot be further compacted into the item's enchanted form, the Dwarven Super Compactor will have no function.
- The Dwarven Super Compactor will not cook the meats of barn animal minions.

### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|

## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Edible Seaweed will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +10 [[Fishing Speed]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Edible Seaweed for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- Despite its name, Edible Seaweed cannot be eaten by the player.

# Eel Shard
Categories: Rare items, Shards
[Eel Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])

Families: Amphibian ([[Shard Families]]), Eel ([[Shard Families]])

[Attribute]

Requirement: Hunting X (10) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: EEL
Tooltip: Eelastic I Increase Pull by +1 when using your Fishing Nets.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R53)
The Eel Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Eelastic]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
An Eel Shard can be obtained from [[Fishing]] in the [[Moonglade Marsh]] with [[Wooden Bait]]. There is a 9.09% (1 in 11 catches) chance for [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] drops to be replaced with one.
### Shop Purchase
An Eel Shard can be purchased from [[Kiara]] for 350,000 [[Coins]]. The player can purchase up to 6 per day, or up to 7 with her [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] unlocked.
### Fusion
An Eel Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Eel Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Eelastic]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Eelastic ([[Attributes]]); Increase Pull by +1–10 when using your Fishing Nets.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
An Eel Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Eel Shard has the following description:
- Their ability to hide in crevices and burrows makes them elusive and difficult to catch.

# Electron Transmitter
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Crystal Hollows
[Electron Transmitter]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ELECTRON_TRANSMITTER
Tooltip: A key component needed by the Professor to repair Automaton Prime in the Lost Precursor City!; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Electron Transmitter is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item and one of the items required to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv71]|[[Automaton]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv67]|[[Automaton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Treasure Chests
Electron Transmitter can also be found as a drop from treasure chests within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. It has a 0.34% chance to roll as a drop in common loot chests, and a 0.29% chance to roll as a drop in rare loot chests.
## Usage
### Sapphire Gemstone Crystal
Bringing an Electron Transmitter and the other 5 [[Automaton]] components to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]] rewards the player with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Precursor Apparatus]]|None|[[Electron Transmitter]] 1x Electron Transmitter 1x [[Superlite Motor]] 1x [[FTX 3070]] 1x [[Robotron Reflector]] 1x [[Control Switch]] 1x [[Synthetic Heart]]|Recipe: [[Control Switch]] + Electron Transmitter + [[FTX 3070]] + [[Robotron Reflector]] + [[Superlite Motor]] + [[Synthetic Heart]] → [[Precursor Apparatus]]|

# Elite Voter's Badge
Categories: Rare items, Accessories
[Elite Voter's Badge]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Source: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) reward as [[Garden Visitors]]
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[VIP Voter's Badge]]
Next →: [[Supreme Voter's Badge]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 3,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VOTER_BADGE_ELITE
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Grants +9 votes in Mayor Elections.; * Co-op Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
Elite Voter's Badge is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
While a mayoral candidate is elected as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], they have a chance to appear as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]]. An Elite Voter's Badge will be rewarded to the player after accepting the offer of 5 unique mayor visitors.
If lost, the Elite Voter's Badge can be repurchased at [[Rusty]] for 50,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
The Elite Voter's Badge grants +9 votes in [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] while in the player's [[Accessory Bag]].
## Trivia
- The Elite Voter's Badge cannot have its rarity upgraded using a [[Recombobulator 3000]].
- Voter's Badge progress is on a per-player basis, meaning a player accepting a mayor visitor will not count towards their co-op members' progress.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Elite Voter's Badge.|
|Jan 26, 2026|Fixed all mayor visitors counting towards Voter's Badge accessory line progress instead of only unique ones.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Elite Voter's Badge.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Fixed all mayor visitors counting towards Voter's Badge accessory line progress instead of only unique ones.|

## Trivia
- [[Auto-pickup]] and [[Looting]] used to not work on an Emperor's Skull.
- The Emperor's Skull's ID is DIVER_FRAGMENT, referencing how it used to only be used to craft [[Diver's Armor]] when first added.

The Enchanted Baked Potato is a [[Rarity|RARE]] crafting item unlocked at [[Potato|Potato VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Potato VII (7) ([[Potato]])|160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Enchanted Potato]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Potato]] x32 ×7 → Enchanted Baked Potato|

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Locust]]|10|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.75%* ([[Pests]])|
### Shiny Pig
Enchanted Baked Potato has a 0.48% (1 in 207.1 pigs) chance to drop from [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] when brought to a [[Shiny Orb]].
## Usage
### Composter Upgrades
Enchanted Baked Potato can be used for Multi Drop upgrades in the [[Composter]]. [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] 911 (14 stacks plus 15) Enchanted Baked Potato is needed to max out Multi Drop.
